## Summary

I've thoroughly searched the codebase for all `ask_user_question` related code. Here's a comprehensive analysis organized by the four areas you requested:

---

## 1. Backend Tool Definition and Handler

**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/mcp_sse.py` (lines 31-222)

### Tool Definition (lines 50-83)
The tool is defined in the MCP server's `list_tools()` function:
- **Name:** `ask_user_question`
- **Description:** "Ask the user a clarifying question with selectable options. Pauses until the user responds."
- **Input Schema:** 
  ```json
  {
    "question": { "type": "string" },
    "options": { 
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "label": { "type": "string" },
          "description": { "type": "string" }
        },
        "required": ["label"]
      }
    },
    "multi_select": { "type": "boolean", "default": false }
  }
  ```

### Tool Handler (lines 210-222)
The handler implementation:
1. **Creates question data** from arguments (question, options, multi_select)
2. **Stores in PendingQuestionStore** via `pending_question_store.create(session_id, question_data)` — returns a unique `question_id`
3. **Awaits user response** via `pending_question_store.wait(session_id, question_id, timeout=300.0)`
4. **Returns result** as JSON containing either:
   - User's answer if responded: `{"answers": [...], "free_text": ...}`
   - Timeout message if no response within 300 seconds: `{"timeout": true, "message": "User did not respond..."}`

**Backend Session ID Management:**
- The `session_id` passed to the handler is the browser session's backend ID (`x_session_id` header)
- This is used as the key in `PendingQuestionStore` to coordinate between tool handler and response endpoint

---

## 2. Response Storage and Conversation History

**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/memory_store.py` (SQLite-backed store)

### Conversation Persistence
Messages are stored in SQLite with:
- **Table:** `messages` (foreign key references `conversations`)
- **Fields:** `id`, `conversation_id`, `role`, `content`, `metadata`, `created_at`, `sort_order`
- **Metadata:** JSON field that stores structured data including segments

### How ask_user_question Responses are Stored
In `backend/app/agent.py` (lines 720-726):

The **SSE generator** (stream_chat function) captures the response and stores segments:
```python
persisted_segments: list[dict] = []
# ...
# After answer text
persisted_segments.append({"type": "answer", "text": answer_text})
# ...
# Finally persisted to SQLite via memory_store.add_message()
meta = assistant_metadata.copy()
if persisted_segments:
    meta["segments"] = persisted_segments
memory_store.add_message(
    conversation_id, "assistant", "".join(text_to_persist),
    metadata=meta if meta else None,
)
```

The `segments` metadata array includes:
- `type: "user_question"` segments with `questionData` and `userAnswer`/`userFreeText` (populated by frontend response)
- Tool calls, tool results, thinking blocks, answers all stored as separate segments

### Response Endpoint
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/backend/app/routes/chat.py` (lines 87-101)

POST `/api/chat/respond`:
```python
@router.post("/api/chat/respond")
async def respond_to_question(
    request: QuestionResponseRequest,
    x_session_id: str = Header(...),
):
    pending = pending_question_store.get_pending(x_session_id)
    if pending is None or pending["question_id"] != request.question_id:
        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": "No pending question found"})
    
    answer = {"answers": request.answers}
    if request.free_text:
        answer["free_text"] = request.free_text
    pending_question_store.respond(x_session_id, request.question_id, answer)
    return {"status": "ok"}
```

This:
1. Looks up pending question by session ID and question ID
2. Calls `pending_question_store.respond()` to set the event and unblock the MCP tool handler
3. The tool handler returns the answer, and the agent continues

---

## 3. Conversation Loading and Restoration

**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx` (lines 775-815)

### Loading Conversations
The `loadMessages` callback:
```typescript
const loadMessages = useCallback((msgs: ChatMessage[], outgoingConversationId?: string | null, incomingConversationId?: string | null) => {
  const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || null;
  const incoming = incomingConversationId || null;
  activeConversationIdRef.current = incoming;

  // Save outgoing conversation's messages to cache (only if not streaming)
  if (outgoing && !streamStatesRef.current.has(outgoing)) {
    const currentMsgs = messagesRef.current;
    if (currentMsgs.length > 0) {
      messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, currentMsgs);
    }
  }

  // Check if incoming conversation has an active stream (re-attachment)
  if (incoming) {
    const streamState = streamStatesRef.current.get(incoming);
    if (streamState) {
      // Re-attach: use live stream messages
      setMessages(streamState.messages);
      setIsStreaming(true);
      return;
    }
  }

  // No active stream -- use backend data or cache
  let finalMsgs = msgs;
  if (incoming) {
    const cached = messagesCacheRef.current.get(incoming);
    if (cached && cached.length > 0 && cached.length > msgs.length) {
      finalMsgs = cached;
    }
    messagesCacheRef.current.delete(incoming);
  }

  setMessages(finalMsgs);
  const hasStreamingMsg = finalMsgs.some(m => m.isStreaming);
  setIsStreaming(hasStreamingMsg);
  sessionIdRef.current = null;
  pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
});
```

### Message Reconstruction from Segments
In the conversation backend API, messages are loaded with their metadata (segments). The frontend reconstructs `ContentSegment[]` from persisted data.

When a conversation with `ask_user_question` segments is loaded:
1. Backend returns `ChatMessage` with `segments` including type `"user_question"`
2. Each `user_question` segment includes `questionData` (question, options, multiSelect) and optionally `userAnswer`/`userFreeText`
3. Frontend displays the segment as answered (collapsed) if `userAnswer` is set, otherwise as an interactive question

---

### SSE Event Handling
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts` (lines 265-281)

The `user_question` SSE event handler:
```typescript
case 'user_question': {
  const rawOptions = (data.options as unknown[]) ?? [];
  const normalizedOptions: UserQuestionOption[] = (Array.isArray(rawOptions) ? rawOptions : []).map(
    (opt: unknown) => {
      if (typeof opt === 'string') return { label: opt };
      if (typeof opt === 'object' && opt !== null && 'label' in opt) return opt as UserQuestionOption;
      return { label: String(opt) };
    }
  );
  callbacks.onUserQuestion?.({
    questionId: data.question_id as string,
    question: data.question as string,
    options: normalizedOptions,
    multiSelect: (data.multi_select as boolean) ?? false,
  });
  break;
}
```

### AgentContext Handler
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx` (lines 355-370)

```typescript
onUserQuestion: (data) => {
  flushNow();
  if (state.currentText.trim()) {
    const segType = state.phase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
    state.segments.push({ type: segType, text: state.currentText });
    state.currentText = '';
  }
  state.segments.push({ type: 'user_question', questionData: data });
  updateMessages(msgs =>
    msgs.map(m =>
      m.id === assistantId
        ? { ...m, segments: [...state.segments] }
        : m
    )
  );
},
```

### UserQuestion Component
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/UserQuestion.tsx`

Component displays:
- **Question text** (rendered with markdown)
- **Options** as clickable buttons (single-select: immediate submit; multi-select: checkbox with submit button)
- **"Other" toggle** for free-text input
- **After answering:** Collapses to show selected answers with visual confirmation

### Integration in MessageBubble
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx` (lines 269-271)

Question segments are filtered and rendered:
```typescript
const questionSegments = hasSegments
  ? message.segments!.filter((s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData)
  : [];
```

Then rendered inline within the message bubble alongside answer text and tool results.

### respondToQuestion Function
**Location:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx` (lines 718-773)

```typescript
const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
  async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
    // Find the stream state that contains this question
    let targetState: StreamState | undefined;
    let targetConvId: string | null = null;

    // Search all active streams for the question
    for (const [cid, state] of streamStatesRef.current) {
      if (state.segments.some(s => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId)) {
        targetState = state;
        targetConvId = cid;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (targetState) {
      const segIdx = targetState.segments.findIndex(
        s => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId
      );
      if (segIdx !== -1) {
        targetState.segments[segIdx] = {
          ...targetState.segments[segIdx],
          userAnswer: answers,
          userFreeText: freeText,
        };
        targetState.messages = targetState.messages.map(m =>
          m.id === targetState!.assistantId
            ? { ...m, segments: [...targetState!.segments] }
            : m
        );
        if (activeConversationIdRef.current === targetConvId) {
          setMessages(targetState.messages);
        }
      }
    }

    // POST to backend to unblock the tool handler
    try {
      await fetch('/api/chat/respond', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          question_id: questionId,
          answers,
          free_text: freeText || null,
        }),
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to respond to question:', e);
    }
  },
  [userSessionId]
);
```

This function:
1. Searches all active streams for the question segment
2. Updates the segment locally with `userAnswer` and `userFreeText`
3. Updates the message to show the answered state (component collapses)
4. POSTs to `/api/chat/respond` to unblock the backend tool handler
5. The agent continues with the user's answer

---

## Key Flow Summary

**During Conversation:**
1. Agent calls `ask_user_question` tool
2. Backend MCP handler stores question in `PendingQuestionStore` and awaits user response
3. SSE generator detects tool call, emits `user_question` event to frontend
4. Frontend `agentService` processes event → `AgentContext.onUserQuestion` creates segment
5. `UserQuestion` component renders inline buttons/options
6. User clicks answer → `respondToQuestion` updates UI and POSTs to `/api/chat/respond`
7. Backend response endpoint calls `pending_question_store.respond()` to unblock the MCP tool handler
8. Tool handler returns answer, agent continues
9. Final assistant message (with segments) is persisted to SQLite with metadata

**Loading Saved Conversation:**
1. Backend returns conversation with messages (including metadata segments)
2. Frontend's `loadMessages` loads them into state
3. `MessageBubble` renders each message, displaying `user_question` segments with already-captured `userAnswer`/`userFreeText`
4. `UserQuestion` component shows answered state (collapsed) instead of interactive buttons

---

   605→                                elif parsed.get("status") == "error":
   606→                                    result_data["error"] = parsed.get("error", "")
   607→                                else:
   608→                                    result_data["output"] = text
   609→                            except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   610→                                result_data["output"] = text
   611→                            if block.get("is_error"):
   612→                                try:
   613→                                    parsed_err = json.loads(text)
   614→                                    result_data["error"] = parsed_err.get("error", text)
   615→                                except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
   616→                                    result_data["error"] = text
   617→                            # Attach chart_spec from captured render_chart
   618→                            # input so the frontend can render the chart.
   619→                            # Only attach when the tool returned success — do
   620→                            # NOT render a chart when the tool returned an
   621→                            # error (e.g. empty traces), as the LLM expects
   622→                            # the chart to have failed and may put its
   623→                            # analysis in thinking instead of text output.
   624→                            if tool_id in tool_chart_specs and "chart_spec" not in result_data and "error" not in result_data:
   625→                                result_data["chart_spec"] = tool_chart_specs[tool_id]
   626→                            # Detect subagent result (Task tool)
   627→                            if name == "sql-analyst":
   628→                                end_data: dict = {"id": tool_id, "name": name}
   629→                                sql_data = subagent_sql_data.get(tool_id, [])
   630→                                if sql_data:
   631→                                    end_data["sql_results"] = [
   632→                                        {k: v for k, v in entry.items() if k != "tool_id"}
   633→                                        for entry in sql_data
   634→                                    ]
   635→                                narrative = subagent_texts.get(tool_id, "") or tool_use_result_text
   636→                                if narrative:
   637→                                    end_data["result"] = narrative
   638→                                # Track subagent_end segment for persistence
   639→                                persisted_segments.append({
   640→                                    "type": "subagent_end",
   641→                                    "subagentId": tool_id,
   642→                                    "subagentName": name,
   643→                                    "text": narrative or None,
   644→                                    "sqlResults": end_data.get("sql_results"),
   645→                                })
   646→                                yield f"event: subagent_end\ndata: {json.dumps(end_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   647→                                continue
   648→                            # Accumulate SQL and chart data for persistence
   649→                            if "columns" in result_data and "rows" in result_data:
   650→                                assistant_metadata["sql_queries"].append({
   651→                                    "sql": result_data.get("sql", ""),
   652→                                    "columns": result_data.get("columns", []),
   653→                                    "rowCount": result_data.get("rowCount", 0),
   654→                                })
   655→                            if "chart_spec" in result_data:
   656→                                assistant_metadata["chart_specs"].append(result_data["chart_spec"])
   657→                            # Update tool segment with result summary
   658→                            for seg in persisted_segments:
   659→                                if seg.get("type") == "tool" and seg.get("toolCallId") == tool_id:
   660→                                    seg["rowCount"] = result_data.get("rowCount")
   661→                                    seg["error"] = result_data.get("error")
   662→                                    if "chart_spec" in result_data:
   663→                                        seg["chart_spec"] = result_data["chart_spec"]
   664→                                    break
   665→                            yield f"event: tool_result\ndata: {json.dumps(result_data, default=str)}\n\n"
   666→
   667→                    # --- Final result ---
   668→                    elif msg_type == "result":
   669→                        actual_session_id = msg.get("session_id") or actual_session_id
   670→                        if msg.get("is_error"):
   671→                            errors = msg.get("errors", [])
   672→                            error_text = msg.get("result") or "; ".join(errors) or "Unknown error"
   673→                            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   674→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   675→                        done_sent = True
   676→
   677→                    # --- Sidecar error (e.g. SDK/CLI crash inside container) ---
   678→                    elif msg_type == "error":
   679→                        error_text = msg.get("message") or "Sidecar error"
   680→                        logger.error("Sidecar reported error: %s", error_text)
   681→                        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': error_text})}\n\n"
   682→                        yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   683→                        done_sent = True
   684→
   685→                    # --- Extract session_id early from system init ---
   686→                    elif msg_type == "system":
   687→                        sys_session = msg.get("session_id")
   688→                        if sys_session:
   689→                            actual_session_id = sys_session
   690→
   691→        # Guard: always send done even if sidecar ended without result message
   692→        if not done_sent:
   693→            logger.warning("Sidecar stream ended without result message; sending done event")
   694→            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': 'Connection to agent was lost. Please try again.'})}\n\n"
   695→            yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   696→
   697→    except Exception as e:
   698→        logger.error("Container agent error: %s", str(e))
   699→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   700→    finally:
   701→        # Persist assistant message even when the client disconnects
   702→        # (e.g. user switched conversations mid-stream).  Without this,
   703→        # the assistant response would be lost on reload.
   704→        text_to_persist = assistant_text_parts or all_text_parts
   705→        if conversation_id and text_to_persist:
   706→            try:
   707→                # Finalize segments: flush remaining buffers
   708→                if seg_thinking_buf:
   709→                    persisted_segments.append({"type": "thinking", "text": "".join(seg_thinking_buf)})
   710→                    seg_thinking_buf.clear()
   711→                # Answer text: use seg_answer_buf (tracks post-thinking text)
   712→                # or assistant_text_parts (tracks post-tool text), or fallback
   713→                answer_text = "".join(seg_answer_buf) if seg_answer_buf else "".join(assistant_text_parts)
   714→                if answer_text:
   715→                    persisted_segments.append({"type": "answer", "text": answer_text})

   223→    case 'subagent_start':
   224→      callbacks.onSubagentStart?.({
   225→        id: data.id as string,
   226→        name: data.name as string,
   227→        prompt: (data.prompt as string) ?? '',
   228→      });
   229→      break;
   230→    case 'subagent_end': {
   231→      type ChartSpec = { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown>; frames?: unknown[] };
   232→      // Backend sends chart_specs (array) for multi-chart support.
   233→      // Fall back to legacy chart_spec (single object) for backwards compat.
   234→      const rawSpecs = data.chart_specs as ChartSpec[] | undefined;
   235→      const legacySpec = data.chart_spec as ChartSpec | undefined;
   236→      const chartSpecs: ChartSpec[] = rawSpecs ?? (legacySpec ? [legacySpec] : []);
   237→
   238→      if (chartSpecs.length > 0) {
   239→        // Emit one callback per chart so each gets its own segment
   240→        for (const spec of chartSpecs) {
   241→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   242→            id: data.id as string,
   243→            name: data.name as string,
   244→            chart_spec: spec,
   245→          });
   246→        }
   247→        // Emit text result separately (if any) so it doesn't get lost
   248→        if (data.result) {
   249→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   250→            id: data.id as string,
   251→            name: data.name as string,
   252→            result: data.result as string,
   253→          });
   254→        }
   255→      } else {
   256→        callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   257→          id: data.id as string,
   258→          name: data.name as string,
   259→          result: (data.result as string) ?? undefined,
   260→          sql_results: (data.sql_results as Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>) ?? undefined,
   261→        });
   262→      }
   263→      break;
   264→    }
   265→    case 'user_question': {
   266→      // Normalize options: the agent may send plain strings or {label, description} objects
   267→      const rawOptions = (data.options as unknown[]) ?? [];
   268→      const normalizedOptions: UserQuestionOption[] = (Array.isArray(rawOptions) ? rawOptions : []).map(
   269→        (opt: unknown) => {
   270→          if (typeof opt === 'string') return { label: opt };
   271→          if (typeof opt === 'object' && opt !== null && 'label' in opt) return opt as UserQuestionOption;
   272→          return { label: String(opt) };
   273→        }
   274→      );
   275→      callbacks.onUserQuestion?.({
   276→        questionId: data.question_id as string,
   277→        question: data.question as string,
   278→        options: normalizedOptions,
   279→        multiSelect: (data.multi_select as boolean) ?? false,
   280→      });

   282→    }

     1→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface BackendMessage {
     4→  id: string;
     5→  role: string;
     6→  content: string;
     7→  metadata: string | null;
     8→}
     9→
    10→/**
    11→ * Convert backend message objects into ChatMessage[] for the UI.
    12→ * Shared by ConversationContext.selectConversation and AgentContext stale-onDone refresh.
    13→ */
    14→export function buildChatMessages(messages: BackendMessage[]): ChatMessage[] {
    15→  return messages.map((msg) => {
    16→    if (msg.role !== 'assistant') {
    17→      return { id: msg.id, role: 'user' as const, content: msg.content };
    18→    }
    19→
    20→    // Try to restore segments from metadata
    21→    let segments: ContentSegment[] | undefined;
    22→    if (msg.metadata) {
    23→      try {
    24→        const meta = typeof msg.metadata === 'string' ? JSON.parse(msg.metadata) : msg.metadata;
    25→        if (Array.isArray(meta?.segments)) {
    26→          segments = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((seg) => {
    27→            const s: ContentSegment = { type: seg.type as ContentSegment['type'] };
    28→            if (seg.text) s.text = seg.text as string;
    29→            if (seg.toolCallId || seg.toolName) {
    30→              s.toolResult = {
    31→                toolCallId: (seg.toolCallId as string) || '',
    32→                toolName: seg.toolName as string,
    33→                sql: (seg.sql as string) || '',
    34→                columns: [],
    35→                rows: [],
    36→                rowCount: (seg.rowCount as number) || 0,
    37→                error: seg.error as string | undefined,
    38→                chart_spec: seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'],
    39→              };
    40→            }
    41→            if (seg.subagentId) s.subagentId = seg.subagentId as string;
    42→            if (seg.subagentName) s.subagentName = seg.subagentName as string;
    43→            if (seg.sqlResults) s.sqlResults = seg.sqlResults as ContentSegment['sqlResults'];
    44→            if (seg.chart_spec && seg.type === 'subagent_end') {
    45→              s.chart_spec = seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'];
    46→            }
    47→            return s;
    48→          });
    49→        }
    50→      } catch {
    51→        // metadata parse failed, fall through to default
    52→      }
    53→    }
    54→
    55→    // Fallback: single answer segment from content
    56→    if (!segments) {
    57→      segments = [{ type: 'answer' as const, text: msg.content }];
    58→    }
    59→
    60→    return {
    61→      id: msg.id,
    62→      role: 'assistant' as const,
    63→      content: msg.content,
    64→      segments,
    65→    };
    66→  });
    67→}
    68→
```

> AGENT

Now I have the complete picture. Let me summarize the root cause.

## Root Cause Found

**Two bugs work together:**

1. **Backend (`agent.py`)**: When `ask_user_question` is called, only a generic `tool` segment is added to `persisted_segments`. The user's answer from the tool_result is never captured in any segment. No `user_question` type segment is ever persisted.

2. **Frontend (`buildChatMessages.ts`)**: The segment reconstruction logic doesn't handle `user_question` segments at all — `questionData`, `userAnswer`, and `userFreeText` are not mapped from the persisted data.

Result: when loading a saved conversation, there's no `user_question` segment with the answer, so nothing renders.

## Implementing the fix

> TOOL

  "old_string": "                                # Detect ask_user_question tool\n                                if \"ask_user_question\" in tool_name:\n                                    from app.pending_questions import pending_question_store\n                                    import asyncio as _asyncio\n                                    for _ in range(50):\n                                        pending = pending_question_store.get_pending(stable_session)\n                                        if pending:\n                                            yield f\"event: user_question\\ndata: {json.dumps({'question_id': pending['question_id'], **pending['data']})}\\n\\n\"\n                                            waiting_for_user = True\n                                            break\n                                        await _asyncio.sleep(0.1)",
  "new_string": "                                # Detect ask_user_question tool\n                                if \"ask_user_question\" in tool_name:\n                                    from app.pending_questions import pending_question_store\n                                    import asyncio as _asyncio\n                                    for _ in range(50):\n                                        pending = pending_question_store.get_pending(stable_session)\n                                        if pending:\n                                            q_id = pending[\"question_id\"]\n                                            q_data = pending[\"data\"]\n                                            yield f\"event: user_question\\ndata: {json.dumps({'question_id': q_id, **q_data})}\\n\\n\"\n                                            waiting_for_user = True\n                                            # Replace generic 'tool' segment with 'user_question' for persistence\n                                            persisted_segments[-1] = {\n                                                \"type\": \"user_question\",\n                                                \"toolCallId\": tool_id,\n                                                \"questionData\": {\n                                                    \"questionId\": q_id,\n                                                    \"question\": q_data.get(\"question\", \"\"),\n                                                    \"options\": [\n                                                        {\"label\": o.get(\"label\", \"\"), **({\"description\": o[\"description\"]} if o.get(\"description\") else {})}\n                                                        for o in q_data.get(\"options\", [])\n                                                    ],\n                                                    \"multiSelect\": q_data.get(\"multi_select\", False),\n                                                },\n                                            }\n                                            break\n                                        await _asyncio.sleep(0.1)"
}

    10→/**
    11→ * Convert backend message objects into ChatMessage[] for the UI.
    12→ * Shared by ConversationContext.selectConversation and AgentContext stale-onDone refresh.
    13→ */
    14→export function buildChatMessages(messages: BackendMessage[]): ChatMessage[] {
    15→  return messages.map((msg) => {
    16→    if (msg.role !== 'assistant') {
    17→      return { id: msg.id, role: 'user' as const, content: msg.content };
    18→    }
    19→
    20→    // Try to restore segments from metadata
    21→    let segments: ContentSegment[] | undefined;
    22→    if (msg.metadata) {
    23→      try {
    24→        const meta = typeof msg.metadata === 'string' ? JSON.parse(msg.metadata) : msg.metadata;
    25→        if (Array.isArray(meta?.segments)) {
    26→          segments = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((seg) => {
    27→            const s: ContentSegment = { type: seg.type as ContentSegment['type'] };
    28→            if (seg.text) s.text = seg.text as string;
    29→            if (seg.toolCallId || seg.toolName) {
    30→              s.toolResult = {
    31→                toolCallId: (seg.toolCallId as string) || '',
    32→                toolName: seg.toolName as string,
    33→                sql: (seg.sql as string) || '',
    34→                columns: [],
    35→                rows: [],
    36→                rowCount: (seg.rowCount as number) || 0,
    37→                error: seg.error as string | undefined,
    38→                chart_spec: seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'],
    39→              };
    40→            }
    41→            if (seg.subagentId) s.subagentId = seg.subagentId as string;
    42→            if (seg.subagentName) s.subagentName = seg.subagentName as string;
    43→            if (seg.sqlResults) s.sqlResults = seg.sqlResults as ContentSegment['sqlResults'];
    44→            if (seg.chart_spec && seg.type === 'subagent_end') {
    45→              s.chart_spec = seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'];
    46→            }
    47→            if (seg.questionData && seg.type === 'user_question') {
    48→              const qd = seg.questionData as Record<string, unknown>;
    49→              s.questionData = {
    50→                questionId: (qd.questionId as string) || '',
    51→                question: (qd.question as string) || '',
    52→                options: (Array.isArray(qd.options) ? qd.options : []) as UserQuestionOption[],
    53→                multiSelect: (qd.multiSelect as boolean) || false,
    54→              } as UserQuestionData;
    55→              if (seg.userAnswer) s.userAnswer = seg.userAnswer as string[];
    56→              if (seg.userFreeText) s.userFreeText = seg.userFreeText as string;
    57→            }
    58→            return s;
    59→          });
    60→        }
    61→      } catch {
    62→        // metadata parse failed, fall through to default
    63→      }
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    // Fallback: single answer segment from content
    67→    if (!segments) {
    68→      segments = [{ type: 'answer' as const, text: msg.content }];
    69→    }
    70→
    71→    return {
    72→      id: msg.id,
    73→      role: 'assistant' as const,
    74→      content: msg.content,
    75→      segments,
    76→    };
    77→  });
    78→}

> AGENT

All types are properly exported. Here's a summary of the fix:

## Summary

**Root cause:** Two-part bug preventing `ask_user_question` responses from persisting across conversation switches.

### Backend fix (`backend/app/agent.py`)

1. **Tool detection** (line ~525): When `ask_user_question` is detected, the generic `tool` segment in `persisted_segments` is replaced with a `user_question` segment containing `questionData` (questionId, question text, options, multiSelect).

2. **Tool result** (line ~606): When the tool result for `ask_user_question` returns, the user's answer (`userAnswer`, `userFreeText`) is extracted from the JSON response and stored on the matching `user_question` segment. The handler then emits the SSE event and `continue`s, skipping the generic SQL/chart parsing.

### Frontend fix (`frontend/src/utils/buildChatMessages.ts`)

1. **Segment reconstruction**: Added mapping for `user_question` segments — `questionData`, `userAnswer`, and `userFreeText` are now restored from persisted metadata.

2. **Guard condition**: Added `seg.type !== 'user_question'` check so that the `toolCallId` on `user_question` segments doesn't accidentally create an unwanted `toolResult` object.

     1→import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
     3→import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
     4→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     5→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment } from '../types';
     6→import { useAgent } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     7→import { InlineQueryResult } from './InlineQueryResult';
     8→import { ChartWidget } from './ChartWidget';
     9→import { UserQuestion } from './UserQuestion';
    10→import './MessageBubble.css';
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Strip chart_spec JSON code blocks from answer text.
    14→ * The orchestrator may include the raw chart spec JSON even though the chart
    15→ * is rendered separately. Remove ```json ... ``` blocks containing chart_spec
    16→ * or Plotly trace data so they don't appear as raw text.
    17→ */
    18→function stripChartSpecBlocks(text: string): string {
    19→  return text.replace(/```(?:json)?\s*\n?\s*\{[\s\S]*?"(?:chart_spec|data)"[\s\S]*?\}\s*\n?\s*```/g, '').trim();
    20→}
    21→
    22→/**
    23→ * Fix missing line breaks before bold section headers in text.
    24→ * The model sometimes concatenates bold headers directly after the previous
    25→ * sentence (e.g. "...for arrays.**Preparing data arrays**"). This inserts
    26→ * paragraph breaks so they render on new lines.
    27→ */
    28→function fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(text: string): string {
    29→  // Insert \n\n before **Header** when preceded by sentence-ending punctuation
    30→  // with no whitespace. The [A-Z] ensures we only match section-header-style
    31→  // bold (starting with a capital letter), avoiding inline bold like **data**.
    32→  return text.replace(/([.!?:])(\*\*[A-Z])/g, '$1\n\n$2');
    33→}
    34→
    35→/** Strip markdown formatting for plain-text preview display. */
    36→function stripMarkdown(text: string): string {
    37→  return text
    38→    .replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, '$1')   // **bold**
    39→    .replace(/__(.+?)__/g, '$1')        // __bold__
    40→    .replace(/\*(.+?)\*/g, '$1')        // *italic*
    41→    .replace(/_(.+?)_/g, '$1')          // _italic_
    42→    .replace(/`(.+?)`/g, '$1')          // `code`
    43→    .replace(/^#+\s+/gm, '');           // # headings
    44→}
    45→
    46→function getLastThinkingLine(segments: ContentSegment[], streamingRemainder: string | undefined, t: (key: string) => string): string {
    47→  // Use streaming remainder if available
    48→  if (streamingRemainder?.trim()) {
    49→    const lines = streamingRemainder.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    50→    const last = stripMarkdown(lines[lines.length - 1] || '');
    51→    return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    52→  }
    53→  // Otherwise use last thinking segment's last line
    54→  for (let i = segments.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    55→    if (segments[i].type === 'thinking' && segments[i].text?.trim()) {
    56→      const lines = segments[i].text!.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    57→      const last = stripMarkdown(lines[lines.length - 1] || '');
    58→      return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    59→    }
    60→  }
    61→  return t('thinking');
    62→}
    63→
    64→function ThinkingBlock({ segments, streamingRemainder, isThinkingPhase, isAgentStreaming, hasAnswer }: {
    65→  segments: ContentSegment[];
    66→  streamingRemainder?: string;
    67→  isThinkingPhase: boolean;
    68→  isAgentStreaming: boolean;
    69→  hasAnswer: boolean;
    70→}) {
    71→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    72→  // All non-answer, non-chart, non-user_question segments go inside the thinking block.
    73→  // subagent_end with sqlResults always stays in thinking; text-only subagent_end without
    74→  // answer goes to the answer block as a preliminary result.
    75→  const thinkingSegments = segments.filter(
    76→    (s) => s.type !== 'answer' && s.type !== 'user_question' && !(s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) && !(s.type === 'subagent_end' && (s.chart_spec || (!s.sqlResults?.length && s.text?.trim() && !hasAnswer)))
    77→  );
    78→  const hasContent = thinkingSegments.some(
    79→    (s) => (s.type === 'thinking' && s.text?.trim()) || (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult) || s.type === 'subagent_start' || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec && (s.text?.trim() || s.sqlResults?.length))
    80→  ) || streamingRemainder?.trim();
    81→
    82→  if (!hasContent) return null;
    83→
    84→  // Show preview only while agent is still working (hidden by CSS when collapsible is open anyway)
    85→  const summary = isAgentStreaming ? getLastThinkingLine(segments, streamingRemainder, t) : '';
    86→
    87→  return (
    88→    <details className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--thinking message-bubble__collapsible" open={isThinkingPhase || (!hasAnswer && !isAgentStreaming) || undefined}>
    89→      <summary className="message-bubble__collapsible-summary">
    90→        <span className="message-bubble__segment-label">{t('thinkingLabel')}</span>
    91→        {summary && <span className="message-bubble__collapsible-preview">{summary}</span>}
    92→      </summary>
    93→      <div className="message-bubble__thinking-body">
    94→        {thinkingSegments.map((seg, i) => {
    95→          if (seg.type === 'thinking' && seg.text?.trim()) {
    96→            return (
    97→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
    98→                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(seg.text)}</ReactMarkdown>
    99→              </div>
   100→            );
   101→          }
   102→          if (seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult) {
   103→            return (
   104→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__tool-segment">
   105→                <InlineQueryResult result={seg.toolResult} />
   106→              </div>
   107→            );
   108→          }
   109→          if (seg.type === 'subagent_start') {
   110→            const displayName = seg.subagentName === 'sql-analyst'
   111→              ? t('sqlAnalystWorking')
   112→              : `${seg.subagentName} working...`;
   113→            return (
   114→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__subagent-indicator">
   115→                <span className="message-bubble__subagent-label">{displayName}</span>
   116→              </div>
   117→            );
   118→          }
   119→          if (seg.type === 'subagent_end' && !seg.chart_spec && (seg.sqlResults?.length || seg.text?.trim())) {
   120→            // Render SQL results using InlineQueryResult — same as direct tool calls
   121→            if (seg.sqlResults?.length) {
   122→              return (
   123→                <div key={i}>
   124→                  {seg.sqlResults.map((sqlResult, qi) => (
   125→                    <div key={qi} className="message-bubble__tool-segment">
   126→                      <InlineQueryResult result={{
   127→                        toolCallId: `${seg.subagentId}-sql-${qi}`,
   128→                        toolName: 'execute_sql',
   129→                        sql: sqlResult.sql,
   130→                        columns: sqlResult.columns ?? [],
   131→                        rows: sqlResult.rows ?? [],
   132→                        rowCount: sqlResult.rowCount ?? 0,
   133→                        error: sqlResult.error,
   134→                      }} />
   135→                    </div>
   136→                  ))}
   137→                </div>
   138→              );
   139→            }
   140→            return (
   141→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   142→                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{seg.text!}</ReactMarkdown>
   143→              </div>
   144→            );
   145→          }
   146→          return null;
   147→        })}
   148→        {streamingRemainder?.trim() && (
   149→          <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   150→            <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(streamingRemainder)}</ReactMarkdown>
   151→          </div>
   152→        )}
   153→      </div>
   154→    </details>
   155→  );
   156→}
   157→
   158→function ErrorBlock({ errorMessage, onRetry }: { errorMessage: string; onRetry?: () => void }) {
   159→  const { t } = useTranslation();
   160→  return (
   161→    <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--error">
   162→      <div className="message-bubble__error-header">{t('errorOccurred')}</div>
   163→      <div className="message-bubble__error-message">{errorMessage}</div>
   164→      {onRetry && (
   165→        <button className="message-bubble__error-retry" onClick={onRetry}>
   166→          {t('tryAgain')}
   167→        </button>
   168→      )}
   169→    </div>
   170→  );
   171→}
   172→
   173→export function MessageBubble({ message, messageIndex }: { message: ChatMessage; messageIndex: number }) {
   174→  const { t } = useTranslation();
   175→  const { isStreaming, editMessage, deleteMessage, sendMessage, messages } = useAgent();
   176→  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
   177→  const [editText, setEditText] = useState(message.content);
   178→  const [isConfirmingDelete, setIsConfirmingDelete] = useState(false);
   179→  const textareaRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
   180→
   181→  const isUser = message.role === 'user';
   182→  const hasSegments = !isUser && message.segments && message.segments.length > 0;
   183→
   184→  useEffect(() => {
   185→    if (isEditing && textareaRef.current) {
   186→      textareaRef.current.focus();
   187→      textareaRef.current.selectionStart = textareaRef.current.value.length;
   188→    }
   189→  }, [isEditing]);
   190→
   191→  const handleEdit = () => {
   192→    setEditText(message.content);
   193→    setIsEditing(true);
   194→    setIsConfirmingDelete(false);
   195→  };
   196→
   197→  const handleCancelEdit = () => {
   198→    setIsEditing(false);
   199→    setEditText(message.content);
   200→  };
   201→
   202→  const handleSaveEdit = () => {
   203→    const trimmed = editText.trim();
   204→    if (!trimmed || trimmed === message.content) {
   205→      handleCancelEdit();
   206→      return;
   207→    }
   208→    setIsEditing(false);
   209→    editMessage(messageIndex, trimmed);
   210→  };
   211→
   212→  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
   213→    setIsConfirmingDelete(false);
   214→    deleteMessage(messageIndex);
   215→  };
   216→
   217→  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
   218→    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
   219→      handleCancelEdit();
   220→    } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && !e.nativeEvent.isComposing) {
   221→      e.preventDefault();
   222→      handleSaveEdit();
   223→    }
   224→  };
   225→
   226→  let streamingRemainder: string | undefined;
   227→  if (hasSegments && message.isStreaming && message.content) {
   228→    const segmentedText = message.segments!
   229→      .filter((s) => s.type === 'thinking' || s.type === 'answer')
   230→      .map((s) => s.text || '')
   231→      .join('');
   232→    const remaining = message.content.slice(segmentedText.length);
   233→    if (remaining.trim()) {
   234→      streamingRemainder = remaining;
   235→    }
   236→  }
   237→
   238→  const hasAnswer = !!hasSegments && message.segments!.some((s) => s.type === 'answer');
   239→  const isInAnswerPhase = message.currentPhase === 'answer';
   240→  const isThinkingPhase = !!message.isStreaming && !hasAnswer && !isInAnswerPhase;
   241→
   242→  const hasCharts = hasSegments && message.segments!.some(
   243→    (s) => (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && s.chart_spec)
   244→  );
   245→
   246→  const answerBlockSegments = hasSegments
   247→    ? message.segments!
   248→        .filter((s) =>
   249→          (s.type === 'answer' && s.text?.trim()) ||
   250→          (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec && !s.sqlResults?.length && s.text?.trim() && !hasAnswer) ||
   251→          (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) ||
   252→          (s.type === 'subagent_end' && s.chart_spec)
   253→        )
   254→        .map((s) => {
   255→          if ((s.type === 'answer' || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec)) && s.text && hasCharts) {
   256→            return { ...s, text: stripChartSpecBlocks(s.text) };
   257→          }
   258→          return s;
   259→        })
   260→        .filter((s) => {
   261→          // Remove text segments that became empty after stripping chart blocks
   262→          if ((s.type === 'answer' || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec)) && !s.text?.trim()) {
   263→            return false;
   264→          }
   265→          return true;
   266→        })
   267→    : [];
   268→
   269→  const questionSegments = hasSegments
   270→    ? message.segments!.filter((s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData)
   271→    : [];
   272→
   273→  return (
   274→    <div className={`message-bubble message-bubble--${message.role}`}>
   275→      <div className="message-bubble__header">
   276→        {isUser ? t('you') : t('assistant')}
   277→        {isUser && !isStreaming && !isEditing && !isConfirmingDelete && (
   278→          <span className="message-bubble__actions">
   279→            <button
   280→              className="message-bubble__action-btn"
   281→              onClick={handleEdit}
   282→              title={t('editMessage')}
   283→            >
   284→              &#9998;
   285→            </button>
   286→            <button
   287→              className="message-bubble__action-btn message-bubble__action-btn--delete"
   288→              onClick={() => setIsConfirmingDelete(true)}
   289→              title={t('deleteMessage')}
   290→            >
   291→              &#128465;
   292→            </button>
   293→          </span>
   294→        )}
   295→      </div>
   296→
   297→      {isUser && isConfirmingDelete && (
   298→        <div className="message-bubble__confirm-delete">
   299→          <span>{t('deleteConfirm')}</span>
   300→          <div className="message-bubble__confirm-actions">
   301→            <button
   302→              className="message-bubble__confirm-btn message-bubble__confirm-btn--delete"
   303→              onClick={handleDeleteConfirm}
   304→            >
   305→              {t('delete')}
   306→            </button>
   307→            <button
   308→              className="message-bubble__confirm-btn"
   309→              onClick={() => setIsConfirmingDelete(false)}
   310→            >
   311→              {t('cancel')}
   312→            </button>
   313→          </div>
   314→        </div>
   315→      )}
   316→
   317→      {isUser && isEditing ? (
   318→        <div className="message-bubble__edit-mode">
   319→          <textarea
   320→            ref={textareaRef}
   321→            className="message-bubble__edit-textarea"
   322→            value={editText}
   323→            onChange={(e) => setEditText(e.target.value)}
   324→            onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
   325→            rows={3}
   326→          />
   327→          <div className="message-bubble__edit-actions">
   328→            <button
   329→              className="message-bubble__edit-btn message-bubble__edit-btn--save"
   330→              onClick={handleSaveEdit}
   331→            >
   332→              {t('saveResend')}
   333→            </button>
   334→            <button
   335→              className="message-bubble__edit-btn"
   336→              onClick={handleCancelEdit}
   337→            >
   338→              {t('cancel')}
   339→            </button>
   340→          </div>
   341→        </div>
   342→      ) : hasSegments ? (
   343→        <div className="message-bubble__segments">
   344→          {!questionSegments.some((s) => !s.userAnswer) && (
   345→            <ThinkingBlock
   346→              segments={message.segments!}
   347→              streamingRemainder={isThinkingPhase ? streamingRemainder : undefined}
   348→              isThinkingPhase={isThinkingPhase}
   349→              isAgentStreaming={!!message.isStreaming}
   350→              hasAnswer={hasAnswer}
   351→            />
   352→          )}
   353→          {questionSegments.map((seg, i) => (
   354→            <div key={`question-${i}`} className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--question">
   355→              <UserQuestion
   356→                questionData={seg.questionData!}
   357→                userAnswer={seg.userAnswer}
   358→                userFreeText={seg.userFreeText}
   359→              />
   360→            </div>
   361→          ))}
   362→          {answerBlockSegments.length > 0 && (
   363→            <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--answer">
   364→              <div className="message-bubble__segment-label message-bubble__segment-label--answer">{t('answer')}</div>
   365→              {answerBlockSegments.map((seg, i) => {
   366→                // Chart segment (tool with chart_spec or subagent_end with chart_spec)
   367→                if ((seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult?.chart_spec) || (seg.type === 'subagent_end' && seg.chart_spec)) {
   368→                  return (
   369→                    <div key={`chart-${i}`} className="message-bubble__chart-in-answer">
   370→                      {seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult ? (
   371→                        <InlineQueryResult result={seg.toolResult!} />
   372→                      ) : seg.chart_spec ? (
   373→                        <ChartWidget data={seg.chart_spec.data} layout={seg.chart_spec.layout} frames={seg.chart_spec.frames} />
   374→                      ) : null}
   375→                    </div>
   376→                  );
   377→                }
   378→                // Text segment (answer or subagent_end without chart_spec)
   379→                return (
   380→                  <div key={`answer-${i}`} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   381→                    <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(seg.text!)}</ReactMarkdown>
   382→                  </div>
   383→                );
   384→              })}
   385→              {/* Render streaming remainder inside the same answer block to avoid duplicate headers */}
   386→              {isInAnswerPhase && streamingRemainder?.trim() && (() => {
   387→                const displayText = hasCharts ? stripChartSpecBlocks(streamingRemainder) : streamingRemainder;
   388→                return displayText?.trim() ? (
   389→                  <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   390→                    <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(displayText)}</ReactMarkdown>
   391→                  </div>
   392→                ) : null;
   393→              })()}
   394→            </div>
   395→          )}
   396→          {/* Only show standalone streaming answer block when no answer segments exist yet */}
   397→          {answerBlockSegments.length === 0 && isInAnswerPhase && streamingRemainder?.trim() && (() => {
   398→            const displayText = hasCharts ? stripChartSpecBlocks(streamingRemainder) : streamingRemainder;
   399→            return displayText?.trim() ? (
   400→              <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--answer">
   401→                <div className="message-bubble__segment-label message-bubble__segment-label--answer">{t('answer')}</div>
   402→                <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   403→                  <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(displayText)}</ReactMarkdown>
   404→                </div>
   405→              </div>
   406→            ) : null;
   407→          })()}
   408→          {message.segments!.filter((s) => s.type === 'error').map((seg, i) => {
   409→            const lastUserMsg = messages.slice().reverse().find((m) => m.role === 'user');
   410→            const handleRetry = lastUserMsg ? () => sendMessage(lastUserMsg.content) : undefined;
   411→            return <ErrorBlock key={`error-${i}`} errorMessage={seg.errorMessage || 'Unknown error'} onRetry={!isStreaming ? handleRetry : undefined} />;
   412→          })}
   413→          {message.isStreaming && !message.content && questionSegments.length === 0 && (
   414→            <span className="message-bubble__typing">{t('thinking')}</span>
   415→          )}
   416→        </div>
   417→      ) : (
   418→        <>
   419→          <div className="message-bubble__content">
   420→            {message.content ? (
   421→              <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(message.content)}</ReactMarkdown>
   422→            ) : message.isStreaming ? (
   423→              <span className="message-bubble__typing">{t('thinking')}</span>
   424→            ) : null}
   425→          </div>
   426→          {message.toolCalls && message.toolCalls.length > 0 && (
   427→            <div className="message-bubble__tools">
   428→              {message.toolCalls.map((tc) => (
   429→                <InlineQueryResult key={tc.toolCallId} result={tc} />
   430→              ))}
   431→            </div>
   432→          )}
   433→        </>
   434→      )}
   435→    </div>
   436→  );
   437→}
   438→

     1→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface BackendMessage {
     4→  id: string;
     5→  role: string;
     6→  content: string;
     7→  metadata: string | null;
     8→}
     9→
    10→/**
    11→ * Convert backend message objects into ChatMessage[] for the UI.
    12→ * Shared by ConversationContext.selectConversation and AgentContext stale-onDone refresh.
    13→ */
    14→export function buildChatMessages(messages: BackendMessage[]): ChatMessage[] {
    15→  return messages.map((msg) => {
    16→    if (msg.role !== 'assistant') {
    17→      return { id: msg.id, role: 'user' as const, content: msg.content };
    18→    }
    19→
    20→    // Try to restore segments from metadata
    21→    let segments: ContentSegment[] | undefined;
    22→    if (msg.metadata) {
    23→      try {
    24→        const meta = typeof msg.metadata === 'string' ? JSON.parse(msg.metadata) : msg.metadata;
    25→        if (Array.isArray(meta?.segments)) {
    26→          segments = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((seg) => {
    27→            const s: ContentSegment = { type: seg.type as ContentSegment['type'] };
    28→            if (seg.text) s.text = seg.text as string;
    29→            if ((seg.toolCallId || seg.toolName) && seg.type !== 'user_question') {
    30→              s.toolResult = {
    31→                toolCallId: (seg.toolCallId as string) || '',
    32→                toolName: seg.toolName as string,
    33→                sql: (seg.sql as string) || '',
    34→                columns: [],
    35→                rows: [],
    36→                rowCount: (seg.rowCount as number) || 0,
    37→                error: seg.error as string | undefined,
    38→                chart_spec: seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'],
    39→              };
    40→            }
    41→            if (seg.subagentId) s.subagentId = seg.subagentId as string;
    42→            if (seg.subagentName) s.subagentName = seg.subagentName as string;
    43→            if (seg.sqlResults) s.sqlResults = seg.sqlResults as ContentSegment['sqlResults'];
    44→            if (seg.chart_spec && seg.type === 'subagent_end') {
    45→              s.chart_spec = seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'];
    46→            }
    47→            if (seg.questionData && seg.type === 'user_question') {
    48→              const qd = seg.questionData as Record<string, unknown>;
    49→              s.questionData = {
    50→                questionId: (qd.questionId as string) || '',
    51→                question: (qd.question as string) || '',
    52→                options: (Array.isArray(qd.options) ? qd.options : []) as UserQuestionOption[],
    53→                multiSelect: (qd.multiSelect as boolean) || false,
    54→              } as UserQuestionData;
    55→              if (seg.userAnswer) s.userAnswer = seg.userAnswer as string[];
    56→              if (seg.userFreeText) s.userFreeText = seg.userFreeText as string;
    57→            }
    58→            return s;
    59→          });
    60→        }
    61→      } catch {
    62→        // metadata parse failed, fall through to default
    63→      }
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    // Fallback: single answer segment from content
    67→    if (!segments) {
    68→      segments = [{ type: 'answer' as const, text: msg.content }];
    69→    }
    70→
    71→    return {
    72→      id: msg.id,
    73→      role: 'assistant' as const,
    74→      content: msg.content,
    75→      segments,
    76→    };
    77→  });
    78→}
    79→

> AGENT

The fix looks correct. Here's what it does:

**The two changes work together:**

1. **Backend** (`agent.py`): Now persists a `user_question` segment (with `questionData` and `userAnswer`) in SQLite metadata, instead of a generic `tool` segment that lost all question/answer data.

2. **Frontend** (`buildChatMessages.ts`): Now reconstructs `questionData`, `userAnswer`, and `userFreeText` from persisted segments when loading a conversation from the backend. Also guards against the `toolCallId` on `user_question` segments incorrectly creating a `toolResult`.

**Important:** Existing conversations already saved before this fix won't have the `user_question` segment in their persisted data. You'll need to test with a **new conversation** that triggers `ask_user_question` after rebuilding. Old conversations' data is already lost from the backend.

To test:
1. Rebuild the frontend and restart the backend
2. Start a new conversation that triggers `ask_user_question`
3. Answer the question
4. Wait for the agent to finish
5. Switch to another conversation, then switch back — the question block with your answer should now persist

     1→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface BackendMessage {
     4→  id: string;
     5→  role: string;
     6→  content: string;
     7→  metadata: string | null;
     8→}
     9→
    10→/**
    11→ * Convert backend message objects into ChatMessage[] for the UI.
    12→ * Shared by ConversationContext.selectConversation and AgentContext stale-onDone refresh.
    13→ */
    14→export function buildChatMessages(messages: BackendMessage[]): ChatMessage[] {
    15→  return messages.map((msg) => {
    16→    if (msg.role !== 'assistant') {
    17→      return { id: msg.id, role: 'user' as const, content: msg.content };
    18→    }
    19→
    20→    // Try to restore segments from metadata
    21→    let segments: ContentSegment[] | undefined;
    22→    if (msg.metadata) {
    23→      try {
    24→        const meta = typeof msg.metadata === 'string' ? JSON.parse(msg.metadata) : msg.metadata;
    25→        if (Array.isArray(meta?.segments)) {
    26→          segments = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((seg) => {
    27→            const s: ContentSegment = { type: seg.type as ContentSegment['type'] };
    28→            if (seg.text) s.text = seg.text as string;
    29→            if ((seg.toolCallId || seg.toolName) && seg.type !== 'user_question') {
    30→              s.toolResult = {
    31→                toolCallId: (seg.toolCallId as string) || '',
    32→                toolName: seg.toolName as string,
    33→                sql: (seg.sql as string) || '',
    34→                columns: [],
    35→                rows: [],
    36→                rowCount: (seg.rowCount as number) || 0,
    37→                error: seg.error as string | undefined,
    38→                chart_spec: seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'],
    39→              };
    40→            }
    41→            if (seg.subagentId) s.subagentId = seg.subagentId as string;
    42→            if (seg.subagentName) s.subagentName = seg.subagentName as string;
    43→            if (seg.sqlResults) s.sqlResults = seg.sqlResults as ContentSegment['sqlResults'];
    44→            if (seg.chart_spec && seg.type === 'subagent_end') {
    45→              s.chart_spec = seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'];
    46→            }
    47→            if (seg.questionData && seg.type === 'user_question') {
    48→              const qd = seg.questionData as Record<string, unknown>;
    49→              s.questionData = {
    50→                questionId: (qd.questionId as string) || '',
    51→                question: (qd.question as string) || '',
    52→                options: (Array.isArray(qd.options) ? qd.options : []) as UserQuestionOption[],
    53→                multiSelect: (qd.multiSelect as boolean) || false,
    54→              } as UserQuestionData;
    55→              if (seg.userAnswer) s.userAnswer = seg.userAnswer as string[];
    56→              if (seg.userFreeText) s.userFreeText = seg.userFreeText as string;
    57→            }
    58→            return s;
    59→          });
    60→        }
    61→      } catch {
    62→        // metadata parse failed, fall through to default
    63→      }
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    // Fallback: single answer segment from content
    67→    if (!segments) {
    68→      segments = [{ type: 'answer' as const, text: msg.content }];
    69→    }
    70→
    71→    return {
    72→      id: msg.id,
    73→      role: 'assistant' as const,
    74→      content: msg.content,
    75→      segments,
    76→    };
    77→  });
    78→}
    79→

     1→import { useState } from 'react';
     2→import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
     3→import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
     4→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     5→import { useAgent } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     6→import type { UserQuestionData } from '../types';
     7→import './UserQuestion.css';
     8→
     9→export function UserQuestion({
    10→  questionData,
    11→  userAnswer,
    12→  userFreeText,
    13→}: {
    14→  questionData: UserQuestionData;
    15→  userAnswer?: string[];
    16→  userFreeText?: string;
    17→}) {
    18→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    19→  const { respondToQuestion } = useAgent();
    20→  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    21→  const [freeText, setFreeText] = useState('');
    22→  const [showFreeText, setShowFreeText] = useState(false);
    23→  const isAnswered = !!userAnswer;
    24→
    25→  const handleOptionClick = (label: string) => {
    26→    if (isAnswered) return;
    27→    if (questionData.multiSelect) {
    28→      setSelected((prev) => {
    29→        const next = new Set(prev);
    30→        if (next.has(label)) next.delete(label);
    31→        else next.add(label);
    32→        return next;
    33→      });
    34→    } else {
    35→      // Single-select: submit immediately
    36→      respondToQuestion(questionData.questionId, [label]);
    37→    }
    38→  };
    39→
    40→  const handleSubmitMulti = () => {
    41→    if (isAnswered) return;
    42→    const answers = [...selected];
    43→    if (showFreeText && freeText.trim()) {
    44→      respondToQuestion(questionData.questionId, answers, freeText.trim());
    45→    } else {
    46→      respondToQuestion(questionData.questionId, answers);
    47→    }
    48→  };
    49→
    50→  const handleSubmitFreeText = () => {
    51→    if (isAnswered || !freeText.trim()) return;
    52→    respondToQuestion(questionData.questionId, [], freeText.trim());
    53→  };
    54→
    55→  if (isAnswered) {
    56→    return (
    57→      <div className="user-question user-question--answered">
    58→        <div className="user-question__label">{t('questionAnswered')}</div>
    59→        <div className="user-question__question"><ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{questionData.question}</ReactMarkdown></div>
    60→        <div className="user-question__selected-answers">
    61→          {userAnswer.map((a, i) => (
    62→            <span key={i} className="user-question__selected-chip">{a}</span>
    63→          ))}
    64→          {userFreeText && (
    65→            <span className="user-question__selected-chip">{userFreeText}</span>
    66→          )}
    67→        </div>
    68→      </div>
    69→    );
    70→  }
    71→
    72→  return (
    73→    <div className="user-question">
    74→      <div className="user-question__label">{t('questionFromAgent')}</div>
    75→      <div className="user-question__question"><ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{questionData.question}</ReactMarkdown></div>
    76→      <div className="user-question__options">
    77→        {questionData.options.map((opt, i) => (
    78→          <button
    79→            key={i}
    80→            className={`user-question__option ${selected.has(opt.label) ? 'user-question__option--selected' : ''}`}
    81→            onClick={() => handleOptionClick(opt.label)}
    82→          >
    83→            {questionData.multiSelect && (
    84→              <span className="user-question__checkbox">
    85→                {selected.has(opt.label) ? '\u2611' : '\u2610'}
    86→              </span>
    87→            )}
    88→            <span className="user-question__option-label">{opt.label}</span>
    89→            {opt.description && (
    90→              <span className="user-question__option-desc">{opt.description}</span>
    91→            )}
    92→          </button>
    93→        ))}
    94→      </div>
    95→      <div className="user-question__free-text-toggle">
    96→        <button
    97→          className="user-question__other-btn"
    98→          onClick={() => setShowFreeText(!showFreeText)}
    99→        >
   100→          {showFreeText ? t('hideOther') : t('other')}
   101→        </button>
   102→      </div>
   103→      {showFreeText && (
   104→        <div className="user-question__free-text">
   105→          <input
   106→            type="text"
   107→            className="user-question__free-text-input"
   108→            placeholder={t('typeYourAnswer')}
   109→            value={freeText}
   110→            onChange={(e) => setFreeText(e.target.value)}
   111→            onKeyDown={(e) => {
   112→              if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.nativeEvent.isComposing) {
   113→                e.preventDefault();
   114→                handleSubmitFreeText();
   115→              }
   116→            }}
   117→          />
   118→          <button
   119→            className="user-question__submit-btn"
   120→            onClick={handleSubmitFreeText}
   121→            disabled={!freeText.trim()}
   122→          >
   123→            {t('submit')}
   124→          </button>
   125→        </div>
   126→      )}
   127→      {questionData.multiSelect && selected.size > 0 && (
   128→        <button className="user-question__submit-btn user-question__submit-multi" onClick={handleSubmitMulti}>
   129→          {t('submit')} ({selected.size})
   130→        </button>
   131→      )}
   132→    </div>
   133→  );
   134→}
   135→
```

  "new_string": "function formatDuration(ms: number): string {\n  if (ms < 1000) return `${ms}ms`;\n  const seconds = Math.round(ms / 1000);\n  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;\n  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n  const remainingSeconds = seconds % 60;\n  return remainingSeconds > 0 ? `${minutes}m ${remainingSeconds}s` : `${minutes}m`;\n}\n\nexport function UserQuestion({\n  questionData,\n  userAnswer,\n  userFreeText,\n  answerDurationMs,\n}: {\n  questionData: UserQuestionData;\n  userAnswer?: string[];\n  userFreeText?: string;\n  answerDurationMs?: number;\n})"
}

Now let me also make sure the `answerDurationMs` is passed through during streaming. The `respondToQuestion` function in AgentContext updates the segment — but the duration comes from the backend (persisted), not the frontend. During streaming, the frontend doesn't know the duration until it's persisted. However, I should also emit it in the SSE `tool_result` event so the frontend can display it immediately.

Let me update the backend to include duration in the SSE event, and update the frontend to capture it during streaming.

> AGENT

The answer timing will be visible when switching back to a conversation. Here's the complete data flow:

**Persistence chain:**
1. Backend records `time.time()` when question SSE event is emitted
2. When user answers and tool_result arrives, backend computes `answerDurationMs = (answered_at - asked_at) * 1000`
3. This is stored in the `user_question` segment in SQLite metadata
4. When loading a conversation, `buildChatMessages` reconstructs `answerDurationMs` from the persisted segment
5. `UserQuestion` component displays it next to "QUESTION ANSWERED" label (e.g., "QUESTION ANSWERED 12s")

**During streaming:**
- The `tool_result` SSE event also includes `answer_duration_ms`
- The frontend captures it and attaches to the `user_question` segment immediately

All changes compile cleanly. You need to rebuild and test with a **new conversation** — old conversations don't have the persisted `user_question` segments.

> AGENT

Good. Here's a summary of what's now persisted and restored:

**Two timing values, both persisted:**

1. **`message.durationMs`** (overall response time, shown in answer block as "Answered in Xs")
   - Backend: `stream_start_time = time.time()` at start, `meta["durationMs"]` computed in `finally` block
   - Frontend: `buildChatMessages` extracts `durationMs` from metadata and sets it on `ChatMessage`

2. **`answerDurationMs`** on `user_question` segments (time user took to answer, shown next to "QUESTION ANSWERED")
   - Backend: `question_asked_at[tool_id]` recorded when question emitted, duration computed when tool_result arrives, stored in `persisted_segments`
   - Frontend: `buildChatMessages` extracts `answerDurationMs` from the `user_question` segment

Both will now survive conversation switches and page refreshes. Rebuild and test with a **new conversation** — old conversations don't have this data in SQLite.
