```
     1→import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
     3→import remarkGfm from 'remark-gfm';
     4→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     5→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment } from '../types';
     6→import { useAgent } from '../hooks/useAgent';
     7→import { InlineQueryResult } from './InlineQueryResult';
     8→import { ChartWidget } from './ChartWidget';
     9→import { UserQuestion } from './UserQuestion';
    10→import './MessageBubble.css';
    11→
    12→/**
    13→ * Strip chart_spec JSON code blocks from answer text.
    14→ * The orchestrator may include the raw chart spec JSON even though the chart
    15→ * is rendered separately. Remove ```json ... ``` blocks containing chart_spec
    16→ * or Plotly trace data so they don't appear as raw text.
    17→ */
    18→function stripChartSpecBlocks(text: string): string {
    19→  return text.replace(/```(?:json)?\s*\n?\s*\{[\s\S]*?"(?:chart_spec|data)"[\s\S]*?\}\s*\n?\s*```/g, '').trim();
    20→}
    21→
    22→/**
    23→ * Fix missing line breaks before bold section headers in text.
    24→ * The model sometimes concatenates bold headers directly after the previous
    25→ * sentence (e.g. "...for arrays.**Preparing data arrays**"). This inserts
    26→ * paragraph breaks so they render on new lines.
    27→ */
    28→function fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(text: string): string {
    29→  // Insert \n\n before **Header** when preceded by sentence-ending punctuation
    30→  // with no whitespace. The [A-Z] ensures we only match section-header-style
    31→  // bold (starting with a capital letter), avoiding inline bold like **data**.
    32→  return text.replace(/([.!?:])(\*\*[A-Z])/g, '$1\n\n$2');
    33→}
    34→
    35→/** Strip markdown formatting for plain-text preview display. */
    36→function stripMarkdown(text: string): string {
    37→  return text
    38→    .replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, '$1')   // **bold**
    39→    .replace(/__(.+?)__/g, '$1')        // __bold__
    40→    .replace(/\*(.+?)\*/g, '$1')        // *italic*
    41→    .replace(/_(.+?)_/g, '$1')          // _italic_
    42→    .replace(/`(.+?)`/g, '$1')          // `code`
    43→    .replace(/^#+\s+/gm, '');           // # headings
    44→}
    45→
    46→function getLastThinkingLine(segments: ContentSegment[], streamingRemainder: string | undefined, t: (key: string) => string): string {
    47→  // Use streaming remainder if available
    48→  if (streamingRemainder?.trim()) {
    49→    const lines = streamingRemainder.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    50→    const last = stripMarkdown(lines[lines.length - 1] || '');
    51→    return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    52→  }
    53→  // Otherwise use last thinking segment's last line
    54→  for (let i = segments.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    55→    if (segments[i].type === 'thinking' && segments[i].text?.trim()) {
    56→      const lines = segments[i].text!.trim().split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim());
    57→      const last = stripMarkdown(lines[lines.length - 1] || '');
    58→      return last.length > 100 ? last.slice(0, 100) + '...' : last;
    59→    }
    60→  }
    61→  return t('thinking');
    62→}
    63→
    64→function ThinkingBlock({ segments, streamingRemainder, isThinkingPhase, isAgentStreaming, hasAnswer }: {
    65→  segments: ContentSegment[];
    66→  streamingRemainder?: string;
    67→  isThinkingPhase: boolean;
    68→  isAgentStreaming: boolean;
    69→  hasAnswer: boolean;
    70→}) {
    71→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    72→  // All non-answer, non-chart, non-user_question segments go inside the thinking block.
    73→  // subagent_end with sqlResults always stays in thinking; text-only subagent_end without
    74→  // answer goes to the answer block as a preliminary result.
    75→  const thinkingSegments = segments.filter(
    76→    (s) => s.type !== 'answer' && s.type !== 'user_question' && !(s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) && !(s.type === 'subagent_end' && (s.chart_spec || (!s.sqlResults?.length && s.text?.trim() && !hasAnswer)))
    77→  );
    78→  const hasContent = thinkingSegments.some(
    79→    (s) => (s.type === 'thinking' && s.text?.trim()) || (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult) || s.type === 'subagent_start' || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec && (s.text?.trim() || s.sqlResults?.length))
    80→  ) || streamingRemainder?.trim();
    81→
    82→  if (!hasContent) return null;
    83→
    84→  // Show preview only while agent is still working (hidden by CSS when collapsible is open anyway)
    85→  const summary = isAgentStreaming ? getLastThinkingLine(segments, streamingRemainder, t) : '';
    86→
    87→  return (
    88→    <details className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--thinking message-bubble__collapsible" open={isThinkingPhase || (!hasAnswer && !isAgentStreaming) || undefined}>
    89→      <summary className="message-bubble__collapsible-summary">
    90→        <span className="message-bubble__segment-label">{t('thinkingLabel')}</span>
    91→        {summary && <span className="message-bubble__collapsible-preview">{summary}</span>}
    92→      </summary>
    93→      <div className="message-bubble__thinking-body">
    94→        {thinkingSegments.map((seg, i) => {
    95→          if (seg.type === 'thinking' && seg.text?.trim()) {
    96→            return (
    97→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
    98→                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(seg.text)}</ReactMarkdown>
    99→              </div>
   100→            );
   101→          }
   102→          if (seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult) {
   103→            return (
   104→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__tool-segment">
   105→                <InlineQueryResult result={seg.toolResult} />
   106→              </div>
   107→            );
   108→          }
   109→          if (seg.type === 'subagent_start') {
   110→            const displayName = seg.subagentName === 'sql-analyst'
   111→              ? t('sqlAnalystWorking')
   112→              : `${seg.subagentName} working...`;
   113→            return (
   114→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__subagent-indicator">
   115→                <span className="message-bubble__subagent-label">{displayName}</span>
   116→              </div>
   117→            );
   118→          }
   119→          if (seg.type === 'subagent_end' && !seg.chart_spec && (seg.sqlResults?.length || seg.text?.trim())) {
   120→            // Render SQL results using InlineQueryResult — same as direct tool calls
   121→            if (seg.sqlResults?.length) {
   122→              return (
   123→                <div key={i}>
   124→                  {seg.sqlResults.map((sqlResult, qi) => (
   125→                    <div key={qi} className="message-bubble__tool-segment">
   126→                      <InlineQueryResult result={{
   127→                        toolCallId: `${seg.subagentId}-sql-${qi}`,
   128→                        toolName: 'execute_sql',
   129→                        sql: sqlResult.sql,
   130→                        columns: sqlResult.columns ?? [],
   131→                        rows: sqlResult.rows ?? [],
   132→                        rowCount: sqlResult.rowCount ?? 0,
   133→                        error: sqlResult.error,
   134→                      }} />
   135→                    </div>
   136→                  ))}
   137→                </div>
   138→              );
   139→            }
   140→            return (
   141→              <div key={i} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   142→                <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{seg.text!}</ReactMarkdown>
   143→              </div>
   144→            );
   145→          }
   146→          return null;
   147→        })}
   148→        {streamingRemainder?.trim() && (
   149→          <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   150→            <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(streamingRemainder)}</ReactMarkdown>
   151→          </div>
   152→        )}
   153→      </div>
   154→    </details>
   155→  );
   156→}
   157→
   158→function ErrorBlock({ errorMessage, onRetry }: { errorMessage: string; onRetry?: () => void }) {
   159→  const { t } = useTranslation();
   160→  return (
   161→    <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--error">
   162→      <div className="message-bubble__error-header">{t('errorOccurred')}</div>
   163→      <div className="message-bubble__error-message">{errorMessage}</div>
   164→      {onRetry && (
   165→        <button className="message-bubble__error-retry" onClick={onRetry}>
   166→          {t('tryAgain')}
   167→        </button>
   168→      )}
   169→    </div>
   170→  );
   171→}
   172→
   173→function formatDuration(ms: number): string {
   174→  if (ms < 1000) return '<1s';
   175→  const seconds = Math.round(ms / 1000);
   176→  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;
   177→  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
   178→  const remainingSeconds = seconds % 60;
   179→  return remainingSeconds > 0 ? `${minutes}m ${remainingSeconds}s` : `${minutes}m`;
   180→}
   181→
   182→export function MessageBubble({ message, messageIndex }: { message: ChatMessage; messageIndex: number }) {
   183→  const { t } = useTranslation();
   184→  const { isStreaming, editMessage, deleteMessage, sendMessage, messages } = useAgent();
   185→  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
   186→  const [editText, setEditText] = useState(message.content);
   187→  const [isConfirmingDelete, setIsConfirmingDelete] = useState(false);
   188→  const textareaRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
   189→
   190→  const isUser = message.role === 'user';
   191→  const hasSegments = !isUser && message.segments && message.segments.length > 0;
   192→
   193→  useEffect(() => {
   194→    if (isEditing && textareaRef.current) {
   195→      textareaRef.current.focus();
   196→      textareaRef.current.selectionStart = textareaRef.current.value.length;
   197→    }
   198→  }, [isEditing]);
   199→
   200→  const handleEdit = () => {
   201→    setEditText(message.content);
   202→    setIsEditing(true);
   203→    setIsConfirmingDelete(false);
   204→  };
   205→
   206→  const handleCancelEdit = () => {
   207→    setIsEditing(false);
   208→    setEditText(message.content);
   209→  };
   210→
   211→  const handleSaveEdit = () => {
   212→    const trimmed = editText.trim();
   213→    if (!trimmed || trimmed === message.content) {
   214→      handleCancelEdit();
   215→      return;
   216→    }
   217→    setIsEditing(false);
   218→    editMessage(messageIndex, trimmed);
   219→  };
   220→
   221→  const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
   222→    setIsConfirmingDelete(false);
   223→    deleteMessage(messageIndex);
   224→  };
   225→
   226→  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
   227→    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
   228→      handleCancelEdit();
   229→    } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && !e.nativeEvent.isComposing) {
   230→      e.preventDefault();
   231→      handleSaveEdit();
   232→    }
   233→  };
   234→
   235→  let streamingRemainder: string | undefined;
   236→  if (hasSegments && message.isStreaming && message.content) {
   237→    const segmentedText = message.segments!
   238→      .filter((s) => s.type === 'thinking' || s.type === 'answer')
   239→      .map((s) => s.text || '')
   240→      .join('');
   241→    const remaining = message.content.slice(segmentedText.length);
   242→    if (remaining.trim()) {
   243→      streamingRemainder = remaining;
   244→    }
   245→  }
   246→
   247→  const hasAnswer = !!hasSegments && message.segments!.some((s) => s.type === 'answer');
   248→  const isInAnswerPhase = message.currentPhase === 'answer';
   249→  const isThinkingPhase = !!message.isStreaming && !hasAnswer && !isInAnswerPhase;
   250→
   251→  const hasCharts = hasSegments && message.segments!.some(
   252→    (s) => (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && s.chart_spec)
   253→  );
   254→
   255→  const answerBlockSegments = hasSegments
   256→    ? message.segments!
   257→        .filter((s) =>
   258→          (s.type === 'answer' && s.text?.trim()) ||
   259→          (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec && !s.sqlResults?.length && s.text?.trim() && !hasAnswer) ||
   260→          (s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.chart_spec) ||
   261→          (s.type === 'subagent_end' && s.chart_spec)
   262→        )
   263→        .map((s) => {
   264→          if ((s.type === 'answer' || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec)) && s.text && hasCharts) {
   265→            return { ...s, text: stripChartSpecBlocks(s.text) };
   266→          }
   267→          return s;
   268→        })
   269→        .filter((s) => {
   270→          // Remove text segments that became empty after stripping chart blocks
   271→          if ((s.type === 'answer' || (s.type === 'subagent_end' && !s.chart_spec)) && !s.text?.trim()) {
   272→            return false;
   273→          }
   274→          return true;
   275→        })
   276→    : [];
   277→
   278→  const questionSegments = hasSegments
   279→    ? message.segments!.filter((s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData)
   280→    : [];
   281→
   282→  // DEBUG
   283→  if (!isUser && questionSegments.length > 0) {
   284→    console.log('[DEBUG MessageBubble] questionSegments:', questionSegments.length, 'hasSegments:', hasSegments, 'isStreaming:', message.isStreaming);
   285→  }
   286→  if (!isUser && hasSegments) {
   287→    const allTypes = message.segments!.map(s => s.type);
   288→    if (allTypes.includes('user_question')) {
   289→      console.log('[DEBUG MessageBubble] segments include user_question, all types:', allTypes, 'questionSegments found:', questionSegments.length);
   290→    }
   291→  }
   292→
   293→  return (
   294→    <div className={`message-bubble message-bubble--${message.role}`}>
   295→      <div className="message-bubble__header">
   296→        {isUser ? t('you') : t('assistant')}
   297→        {isUser && !isStreaming && !isEditing && !isConfirmingDelete && (
   298→          <span className="message-bubble__actions">
   299→            <button
   300→              className="message-bubble__action-btn"
   301→              onClick={handleEdit}
   302→              title={t('editMessage')}
   303→            >
   304→              &#9998;
   305→            </button>
   306→            <button
   307→              className="message-bubble__action-btn message-bubble__action-btn--delete"
   308→              onClick={() => setIsConfirmingDelete(true)}
   309→              title={t('deleteMessage')}
   310→            >
   311→              &#128465;
   312→            </button>
   313→          </span>
   314→        )}
   315→      </div>
   316→
   317→      {isUser && isConfirmingDelete && (
   318→        <div className="message-bubble__confirm-delete">
   319→          <span>{t('deleteConfirm')}</span>
   320→          <div className="message-bubble__confirm-actions">
   321→            <button
   322→              className="message-bubble__confirm-btn message-bubble__confirm-btn--delete"
   323→              onClick={handleDeleteConfirm}
   324→            >
   325→              {t('delete')}
   326→            </button>
   327→            <button
   328→              className="message-bubble__confirm-btn"
   329→              onClick={() => setIsConfirmingDelete(false)}
   330→            >
   331→              {t('cancel')}
   332→            </button>
   333→          </div>
   334→        </div>
   335→      )}
   336→
   337→      {isUser && isEditing ? (
   338→        <div className="message-bubble__edit-mode">
   339→          <textarea
   340→            ref={textareaRef}
   341→            className="message-bubble__edit-textarea"
   342→            value={editText}
   343→            onChange={(e) => setEditText(e.target.value)}
   344→            onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
   345→            rows={3}
   346→          />
   347→          <div className="message-bubble__edit-actions">
   348→            <button
   349→              className="message-bubble__edit-btn message-bubble__edit-btn--save"
   350→              onClick={handleSaveEdit}
   351→            >
   352→              {t('saveResend')}
   353→            </button>
   354→            <button
   355→              className="message-bubble__edit-btn"
   356→              onClick={handleCancelEdit}
   357→            >
   358→              {t('cancel')}
   359→            </button>
   360→          </div>
   361→        </div>
   362→      ) : hasSegments ? (
   363→        <div className="message-bubble__segments">
   364→          {!questionSegments.some((s) => !s.userAnswer) && (
   365→            <ThinkingBlock
   366→              segments={message.segments!}
   367→              streamingRemainder={isThinkingPhase ? streamingRemainder : undefined}
   368→              isThinkingPhase={isThinkingPhase}
   369→              isAgentStreaming={!!message.isStreaming}
   370→              hasAnswer={hasAnswer}
   371→            />
   372→          )}
   373→          {questionSegments.map((seg, i) => (
   374→            <div key={`question-${i}`} className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--question" style={{ border: '3px solid red', background: 'rgba(255,0,0,0.1)' }}>
   375→              <UserQuestion
   376→                questionData={seg.questionData!}
   377→                userAnswer={seg.userAnswer}
   378→                userFreeText={seg.userFreeText}
   379→                answerDurationMs={seg.answerDurationMs}
   380→              />
   381→            </div>
   382→          ))}
   383→          {answerBlockSegments.length > 0 && (
   384→            <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--answer">
   385→              <div className="message-bubble__segment-label message-bubble__segment-label--answer">{t('answer')}</div>
   386→              {answerBlockSegments.map((seg, i) => {
   387→                // Chart segment (tool with chart_spec or subagent_end with chart_spec)
   388→                if ((seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult?.chart_spec) || (seg.type === 'subagent_end' && seg.chart_spec)) {
   389→                  return (
   390→                    <div key={`chart-${i}`} className="message-bubble__chart-in-answer">
   391→                      {seg.type === 'tool' && seg.toolResult ? (
   392→                        <InlineQueryResult result={seg.toolResult!} />
   393→                      ) : seg.chart_spec ? (
   394→                        <ChartWidget data={seg.chart_spec.data} layout={seg.chart_spec.layout} frames={seg.chart_spec.frames} />
   395→                      ) : null}
   396→                    </div>
   397→                  );
   398→                }
   399→                // Text segment (answer or subagent_end without chart_spec)
   400→                return (
   401→                  <div key={`answer-${i}`} className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   402→                    <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(seg.text!)}</ReactMarkdown>
   403→                  </div>
   404→                );
   405→              })}
   406→              {/* Render streaming remainder inside the same answer block to avoid duplicate headers */}
   407→              {isInAnswerPhase && streamingRemainder?.trim() && (() => {
   408→                const displayText = hasCharts ? stripChartSpecBlocks(streamingRemainder) : streamingRemainder;
   409→                return displayText?.trim() ? (
   410→                  <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   411→                    <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(displayText)}</ReactMarkdown>
   412→                  </div>
   413→                ) : null;
   414→              })()}
   415→              {!message.isStreaming && message.durationMs != null && (
   416→                <div className="message-bubble__duration">
   417→                  {t('answeredIn', { time: formatDuration(message.durationMs) })}
   418→                </div>
   419→              )}
   420→            </div>
   421→          )}
   422→          {/* Only show standalone streaming answer block when no answer segments exist yet */}
   423→          {answerBlockSegments.length === 0 && isInAnswerPhase && streamingRemainder?.trim() && (() => {
   424→            const displayText = hasCharts ? stripChartSpecBlocks(streamingRemainder) : streamingRemainder;
   425→            return displayText?.trim() ? (
   426→              <div className="message-bubble__segment message-bubble__segment--answer">
   427→                <div className="message-bubble__segment-label message-bubble__segment-label--answer">{t('answer')}</div>
   428→                <div className="message-bubble__segment-content">
   429→                  <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(displayText)}</ReactMarkdown>
   430→                </div>
   431→              </div>
   432→            ) : null;
   433→          })()}
   434→          {message.segments!.filter((s) => s.type === 'error').map((seg, i) => {
   435→            const lastUserMsg = messages.slice().reverse().find((m) => m.role === 'user');
   436→            const handleRetry = lastUserMsg ? () => sendMessage(lastUserMsg.content) : undefined;
   437→            return <ErrorBlock key={`error-${i}`} errorMessage={seg.errorMessage || 'Unknown error'} onRetry={!isStreaming ? handleRetry : undefined} />;
   438→          })}
   439→          {message.isStreaming && !message.content && questionSegments.length === 0 && (
   440→            <span className="message-bubble__typing">{t('thinking')}</span>
   441→          )}
   442→        </div>
   443→      ) : (
   444→        <>
   445→          <div className="message-bubble__content">
   446→            {message.content ? (
   447→              <ReactMarkdown remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}>{fixBoldHeaderLineBreaks(message.content)}</ReactMarkdown>
   448→            ) : message.isStreaming ? (
   449→              <span className="message-bubble__typing">{t('thinking')}</span>
   450→            ) : null}
   451→          </div>
   452→          {message.toolCalls && message.toolCalls.length > 0 && (
   453→            <div className="message-bubble__tools">
   454→              {message.toolCalls.map((tc) => (
   455→                <InlineQueryResult key={tc.toolCallId} result={tc} />
   456→              ))}
   457→            </div>
   458→          )}
   459→        </>
   460→      )}
   461→    </div>
   462→  );
   463→}
   464→
```

```
     1→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface BackendMessage {
     4→  id: string;
     5→  role: string;
     6→  content: string;
     7→  metadata: string | null;
     8→}
     9→
    10→/**
    11→ * Convert backend message objects into ChatMessage[] for the UI.
    12→ * Shared by ConversationContext.selectConversation and AgentContext stale-onDone refresh.
    13→ */
    14→export function buildChatMessages(messages: BackendMessage[]): ChatMessage[] {
    15→  return messages.map((msg) => {
    16→    if (msg.role !== 'assistant') {
    17→      return { id: msg.id, role: 'user' as const, content: msg.content };
    18→    }
    19→
    20→    // Try to restore segments from metadata
    21→    let segments: ContentSegment[] | undefined;
    22→    let durationMs: number | undefined;
    23→    if (msg.metadata) {
    24→      try {
    25→        const meta = typeof msg.metadata === 'string' ? JSON.parse(msg.metadata) : msg.metadata;
    26→        if (typeof meta?.durationMs === 'number') durationMs = meta.durationMs;
    27→        if (Array.isArray(meta?.segments)) {
    28→          // DEBUG: log segment types from backend
    29→          const segTypes = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map(s => s.type);
    30→          console.log('[DEBUG buildChatMessages] segment types:', segTypes);
    31→          const uqSegs = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).filter(s => s.type === 'user_question');
    32→          if (uqSegs.length > 0) console.log('[DEBUG buildChatMessages] user_question segments:', uqSegs);
    33→          segments = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((seg) => {
    34→            const s: ContentSegment = { type: seg.type as ContentSegment['type'] };
    35→            if (seg.text) s.text = seg.text as string;
    36→            if ((seg.toolCallId || seg.toolName) && seg.type !== 'user_question') {
    37→              s.toolResult = {
    38→                toolCallId: (seg.toolCallId as string) || '',
    39→                toolName: seg.toolName as string,
    40→                sql: (seg.sql as string) || '',
    41→                columns: [],
    42→                rows: [],
    43→                rowCount: (seg.rowCount as number) || 0,
    44→                error: seg.error as string | undefined,
    45→                chart_spec: seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'],
    46→              };
    47→            }
    48→            if (seg.subagentId) s.subagentId = seg.subagentId as string;
    49→            if (seg.subagentName) s.subagentName = seg.subagentName as string;
    50→            if (seg.sqlResults) s.sqlResults = seg.sqlResults as ContentSegment['sqlResults'];
    51→            if (seg.chart_spec && seg.type === 'subagent_end') {
    52→              s.chart_spec = seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'];
    53→            }
    54→            if (seg.questionData && seg.type === 'user_question') {
    55→              const qd = seg.questionData as Record<string, unknown>;
    56→              s.questionData = {
    57→                questionId: (qd.questionId as string) || '',
    58→                question: (qd.question as string) || '',
    59→                options: (Array.isArray(qd.options) ? qd.options : []) as UserQuestionOption[],
    60→                multiSelect: (qd.multiSelect as boolean) || false,
    61→              } as UserQuestionData;
    62→              if (seg.userAnswer) s.userAnswer = seg.userAnswer as string[];
    63→              if (seg.userFreeText) s.userFreeText = seg.userFreeText as string;
    64→              if (seg.answerDurationMs) s.answerDurationMs = seg.answerDurationMs as number;
    65→            }
    66→            return s;
    67→          });
    68→        }
    69→      } catch {
    70→        // metadata parse failed, fall through to default
    71→      }
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    // Fallback: single answer segment from content
    75→    if (!segments) {
    76→      segments = [{ type: 'answer' as const, text: msg.content }];
    77→    }
    78→
    79→    return {
    80→      id: msg.id,
    81→      role: 'assistant' as const,
    82→      content: msg.content,
    83→      segments,
    84→      ...(durationMs != null && { durationMs }),
    85→    };
    86→  });
    87→}
    88→
```

```
   739→        # Guard: always send done even if sidecar ended without result message
   740→        if not done_sent:
   741→            logger.warning("Sidecar stream ended without result message; sending done event")
   742→            yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': 'Connection to agent was lost. Please try again.'})}\n\n"
   743→            yield f"event: done\ndata: {json.dumps({'session_id': actual_session_id})}\n\n"
   744→
   745→    except Exception as e:
   746→        logger.error("Container agent error: %s", str(e))
   747→        yield f"event: error\ndata: {json.dumps({'message': str(e)})}\n\n"
   748→    finally:
   749→        # Persist assistant message even when the client disconnects
```

> AGENT

All changes are complete. Here's a summary of what was done:

**1. Answered `user_question` now renders INSIDE the thinking block** (the main fix):
- `ThinkingBlock` filter changed from excluding all `user_question` to only excluding *unanswered* ones (`!(s.type === 'user_question' && !s.userAnswer)`)
- Added `UserQuestion` rendering case inside ThinkingBlock's segment map
- Outer `questionSegments` now only shows *unanswered* questions (for live interaction)

**2. SQL query results persist across conversation switches**:
- Backend (`agent.py`): Now saves `columns` and `rows` data to the persisted tool segment (previously only saved `rowCount`)
- Frontend (`buildChatMessages.ts`): Reconstructs `columns` and `rows` from metadata instead of hardcoding empty arrays

**3. Debug code removed**:
- Removed 5 `print(..., flush=True)` debug statements from `agent.py`
- Removed 4 `console.log` debug statements from `buildChatMessages.ts`
- Removed `console.log` debug statements and red border inline style from `MessageBubble.tsx`

Note: The SQL query fix will only take effect for **new** conversations — existing conversations in SQLite don't have `columns`/`rows` in their metadata, so those will still show empty tables.

```
     1→import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     3→import type { ToolCallResult } from '../types';
     4→import './InlineQueryResult.css';
     5→import { ChartWidget } from './ChartWidget';
     6→
     7→function CopyButton({ text }: { text: string }) {
     8→  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
     9→
    10→  const handleCopy = useCallback(async () => {
    11→    try {
    12→      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    13→      setCopied(true);
    14→      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);
    15→    } catch {
    16→      // fallback
    17→    }
    18→  }, [text]);
    19→
    20→  return (
    21→    <button
    22→      className="inline-query__copy-btn"
    23→      onClick={handleCopy}
    24→      title={copied ? 'Copied!' : 'Copy'}
    25→    >
    26→      {copied ? (
    27→        <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
    28→          <polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12" />
    29→        </svg>
    30→      ) : (
    31→        <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round">
    32→          <rect x="9" y="9" width="13" height="13" rx="2" ry="2" />
    33→          <path d="M5 15H4a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h9a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v1" />
    34→        </svg>
    35→      )}
    36→    </button>
    37→  );
    38→}
    39→
    40→const MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS = 20;
    41→
    42→function getToolDisplayName(result: ToolCallResult, t: (key: string) => string): string {
    43→  const name = result.toolName || '';
    44→  if (name.includes('execute_sql')) return t('sqlQuery');
    45→  if (name === 'Bash') return t('bash');
    46→  if (name === 'Read') return t('readFile');
    47→  if (name === 'Write') return t('writeFile');
    48→  if (name === 'Edit') return t('editFile');
    49→  if (name.startsWith('mcp__')) {
    50→    const parts = name.split('__');
    51→    return parts[parts.length - 1] || name;
    52→  }
    53→  return name || 'Tool';
    54→}
    55→
    56→function getToolInputDisplay(result: ToolCallResult): string | null {
    57→  if (result.sql) return result.sql;
    58→  if (result.command) return result.command;
    59→  if (result.toolInput) {
    60→    const entries = Object.entries(result.toolInput);
    61→    if (entries.length === 0) return null;
    62→    // For single-value inputs, show just the value
    63→    if (entries.length === 1) {
    64→      const val = entries[0][1];
    65→      if (val === null || val === undefined) return '';
    66→      if (typeof val === 'object') return JSON.stringify(val, null, 2);
    67→      return String(val);
    68→    }
    69→    return JSON.stringify(result.toolInput, null, 2);
    70→  }
    71→  return null;
    72→}
    73→
    74→function isSQL(result: ToolCallResult): boolean {
    75→  return !!result.sql && (result.toolName?.includes('execute_sql') ?? false);
    76→}
    77→
    78→export function InlineQueryResult({ result }: { result: ToolCallResult }) {
    79→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    80→  const displayName = getToolDisplayName(result, t);
    81→  const inputDisplay = getToolInputDisplay(result);
    82→  const isSQLTool = isSQL(result);
    83→  const hasStructuredData = result.columns.length > 0;
    84→  const displayRows = result.rows.slice(0, MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS);
    85→
    86→  // Render chart if chart_spec is present
    87→  if (result.chart_spec) {
    88→    return (
    89→      <div className="inline-query inline-query--chart">
    90→        <div className="inline-query__label inline-query__label--generic">
    91→          {(result.chart_spec?.layout?.title as string) || getToolDisplayName(result, t)}
    92→          <CopyButton text={JSON.stringify(result.chart_spec, null, 2)} />
    93→        </div>
    94→        <ChartWidget
    95→          data={result.chart_spec.data}
    96→          layout={result.chart_spec.layout}
    97→          frames={result.chart_spec.frames}
    98→        />
    99→      </div>
   100→    );
   101→  }
   102→
   103→  // Determine border color class
   104→  let variantClass = '';
   105→  if (result.error) variantClass = 'inline-query--error';
   106→  else if (result.command) variantClass = 'inline-query--bash';
   107→  else if (!isSQLTool && !result.sql) variantClass = 'inline-query--generic';
   108→
   109→  return (
   110→    <div className={`inline-query ${variantClass}`}>
   111→      {/* Tool label */}
   112→      <div className={`inline-query__label ${isSQLTool ? 'inline-query__label--sql' : result.command ? 'inline-query__label--bash' : 'inline-query__label--generic'}`}>
   113→        {displayName}
   114→      </div>
   115→
   116→      {/* Tool input */}
   117→      {inputDisplay && (
   118→        <div className="inline-query__sql">
   119→          <CopyButton text={inputDisplay} />
   120→          <code>{inputDisplay}</code>
   121→        </div>
   122→      )}
   123→
   124→      {/* Error display */}
   125→      {result.error && (
   126→        <div className="inline-query__error">{result.error}</div>
   127→      )}
   128→
   129→      {/* SQL structured results */}
   130→      {!result.error && isSQLTool && hasStructuredData && (
   131→        <>
   132→          <div className="inline-query__table-wrapper">
   133→            <table className="inline-query__table">
   134→              <thead>
   135→                <tr>
   136→                  {result.columns.map((col) => (
   137→                    <th key={col}>{col}</th>
   138→                  ))}
   139→                </tr>
   140→              </thead>
   141→              <tbody>
   142→                {displayRows.map((row, i) => (
   143→                  <tr key={i}>
   144→                    {result.columns.map((col) => (
   145→                      <td key={col}>{String(row[col] ?? '')}</td>
   146→                    ))}
   147→                  </tr>
   148→                ))}
   149→              </tbody>
   150→            </table>
   151→          </div>
   152→          <div className="inline-query__meta">
   153→            {t('rowCount', { count: result.rowCount })}
   154→            {result.rowCount > MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS && ` ${t('showingCount', { count: MAX_DISPLAY_ROWS })}`}
   155→          </div>
   156→        </>
   157→      )}
   158→
   159→      {/* Text output for non-SQL tools */}
   160→      {!result.error && result.output && (
   161→        <div className="inline-query__output">
   162→          <pre>{result.output}</pre>
   163→        </div>
   164→      )}
   165→
   166→      {/* Raw content fallback */}
   167→      {!result.error && !hasStructuredData && !result.output && result.rawContent && (
   168→        <div className="inline-query__raw">
   169→          <pre>{result.rawContent}</pre>
   170→        </div>
   171→      )}
   172→
   173→      {/* Pending state */}
   174→      {!result.error && !hasStructuredData && !result.output && !result.rawContent && inputDisplay && (
   175→        <div className="inline-query__meta">{t('executing')}</div>
   176→      )}
   177→    </div>
   178→  );
   179→}
   180→
```

> AGENT

Now I see. The screenshot shows:
1. Tool name "ask_user_question" as header
2. The JSON input (question, options, multi_select) 
3. The answer output `{"answers": ["Survival rate by sex"]}`

This is rendered by `InlineQueryResult` as a `tool` type segment. The problem: backend **replaces** the `tool` segment with `user_question`, so on reload the tool block is gone.

The fix: **don't replace** the tool segment — **append** the `user_question` segment after it. And when the answer comes back, update **both** segments.

```
     1→import type { ChatMessage, ContentSegment, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface BackendMessage {
     4→  id: string;
     5→  role: string;
     6→  content: string;
     7→  metadata: string | null;
     8→}
     9→
    10→/**
    11→ * Convert backend message objects into ChatMessage[] for the UI.
    12→ * Shared by ConversationContext.selectConversation and AgentContext stale-onDone refresh.
    13→ */
    14→export function buildChatMessages(messages: BackendMessage[]): ChatMessage[] {
    15→  return messages.map((msg) => {
    16→    if (msg.role !== 'assistant') {
    17→      return { id: msg.id, role: 'user' as const, content: msg.content };
    18→    }
    19→
    20→    // Try to restore segments from metadata
    21→    let segments: ContentSegment[] | undefined;
    22→    let durationMs: number | undefined;
    23→    if (msg.metadata) {
    24→      try {
    25→        const meta = typeof msg.metadata === 'string' ? JSON.parse(msg.metadata) : msg.metadata;
    26→        if (typeof meta?.durationMs === 'number') durationMs = meta.durationMs;
    27→        if (Array.isArray(meta?.segments)) {
    28→          segments = (meta.segments as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((seg) => {
    29→            const s: ContentSegment = { type: seg.type as ContentSegment['type'] };
    30→            if (seg.text) s.text = seg.text as string;
    31→            if ((seg.toolCallId || seg.toolName) && seg.type !== 'user_question') {
    32→              s.toolResult = {
    33→                toolCallId: (seg.toolCallId as string) || '',
    34→                toolName: seg.toolName as string,
    35→                sql: (seg.sql as string) || '',
    36→                columns: (Array.isArray(seg.columns) ? seg.columns : []) as string[],
    37→                rows: (Array.isArray(seg.rows) ? seg.rows : []) as Record<string, unknown>[],
    38→                rowCount: (seg.rowCount as number) || 0,
    39→                error: seg.error as string | undefined,
    40→                chart_spec: seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'],
    41→              };
    42→            }
    43→            if (seg.subagentId) s.subagentId = seg.subagentId as string;
    44→            if (seg.subagentName) s.subagentName = seg.subagentName as string;
    45→            if (seg.sqlResults) s.sqlResults = seg.sqlResults as ContentSegment['sqlResults'];
    46→            if (seg.chart_spec && seg.type === 'subagent_end') {
    47→              s.chart_spec = seg.chart_spec as ContentSegment['chart_spec'];
    48→            }
    49→            if (seg.questionData && seg.type === 'user_question') {
    50→              const qd = seg.questionData as Record<string, unknown>;
    51→              s.questionData = {
    52→                questionId: (qd.questionId as string) || '',
    53→                question: (qd.question as string) || '',
    54→                options: (Array.isArray(qd.options) ? qd.options : []) as UserQuestionOption[],
    55→                multiSelect: (qd.multiSelect as boolean) || false,
    56→              } as UserQuestionData;
    57→              if (seg.userAnswer) s.userAnswer = seg.userAnswer as string[];
    58→              if (seg.userFreeText) s.userFreeText = seg.userFreeText as string;
    59→              if (seg.answerDurationMs) s.answerDurationMs = seg.answerDurationMs as number;
    60→            }
    61→            return s;
    62→          });
    63→        }
    64→      } catch {
    65→        // metadata parse failed, fall through to default
    66→      }
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    // Fallback: single answer segment from content
    70→    if (!segments) {
    71→      segments = [{ type: 'answer' as const, text: msg.content }];
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    return {
    75→      id: msg.id,
    76→      role: 'assistant' as const,
    77→      content: msg.content,
    78→      segments,
    79→      ...(durationMs != null && { durationMs }),
    80→    };
    81→  });
    82→}
    83→
```
